# Методология системного аудита валидатора

Версия документа: 1.1. Основание: инструкция пользователя от 2026-09-20.
Это проектное приложение к [общему регламенту взаимодействия](docs/AGENT_COLLABORATION_PROTOCOL.md),
а не универсальная инструкция для других задач. Формы передачи:
[AGENT_COLLABORATION_TEMPLATES.md](docs/AGENT_COLLABORATION_TEMPLATES.md).
Исходный код для начальной инвентаризации: `e5eede6`.
Статус: **IN_PROGRESS — полнота инвентаризации и покрытия ещё не подтверждена**.

## 1.1 Активный срез аудита — resume evidence integrity (2026-09-28)

Этот ограниченный срез относится к Gate B1/B2 и предшествует исправлению
`cmd_resume`. Проверяемая база: harness commit `10108e9cf58f868bb147f99ba8b9e5f6baa9a4b7`,
`host/runner.py` v1.5 (SHA-256
`6388b82f062dfc9fab405d2820b6d86600449b4452f8610bb61bc70a74c51085`), product
`.216` / versionCode 335. Нормативы: этот документ §§1–7 (I07, I21–I23),
`docs/AGENT_COLLABORATION_PROTOCOL.md` §§3–5 и существующая схема
`resume_attempts.json` в `validate_dir`.

**Сценарий пользователя:** инженер возобновляет прерванный DEV/qualification цикл.
До любой команды устройству runner обязан сохранить исходные evidence/attempts,
проверить их целостность и подтвердить, что запускает тот же цикл на том же
устройстве и APK. Ошибку чтения нельзя превращать в пустую историю или считать
отсутствием evidence.

**Найденные места для проверки одним классом (P/F/C/E):**

| Место в baseline `host/runner.py` | Роль и статус проверки |
|---|---|
| `cmd_resume` ~2638–2639 | Исключение при чтении любого cycle JSON игнорируется; проверяется, может ли runner начать ADB/device работу до последующего `validate_dir`. |
| `cmd_resume` ~2696–2704 | Существующий `resume_attempts.json` при parse/read exception заменяется `[]`; это может стереть журнал при следующей записи. Дефект подтверждён чтением кода; runtime regression ещё не запускался. |
| `build_s1_aggregate_from_disk` ~2353–2371 | Metadata candidate files перебираются с fallback. Это допустимо только для необязательного/восстановимого источника; malformed JSON остаётся видимым `validate_dir`, а старый aggregate пересобирается из per-run evidence. Проверить побочные действия и сохранить как исключение либо включить в исправление на основе теста. |
| `cmd_package` ~2880–2894 | Копирование raw log пропускает исключение, но последующий `validate_dir` проверяет каждую JSON ссылку и fail-closed при отсутствующем mandatory log; исходный цикл остаётся нетронут. Защищённый аналог при подтверждении регрессией валидатора. |
| `validate_dir` ~797–799, `cmd_verify_gates` ~2249–2255, evidence-log readers ~335–337 и ~764–766, dump reader ~1135–1140 | Parse/read failure заканчивается ошибкой, без fallback к PASS. Защищённые аналоги. |
| `cmd_resume` ~2717–2727 | Повреждённые per-run файлы сохраняются и записываются как failed attempt; не удалять и не заменять это поведение. Защищённый аналог. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case` ~402–414 | Ошибка разбора PID игнорируется только до обязательной проверки `pid is None`, которая вызывает fail-closed; не принимать текстовый маркер за успешно проверенную смерть. Защищённый аналог. |

**Положительные/отрицательные сценарии до кода:**

1. Нет журнала попыток: resume создаёт новый список попыток; существующий end-to-end сценарий остаётся валидным.
2. Корректный непустой журнал: прежние записи логически сохраняются, новая попытка добавляется, outcome меняется только после `validate_dir`.
3. Malformed/truncated attempts JSON, top-level не-list, entry не-object, отсутствующий обязательный ключ, недопустимый mode/outcome или I/O read failure: resume завершает работу до ADB/device-команды; исходные байты остаются неизменными.
4. Повреждённый cycle JSON при наличии другого пригодного identity JSON: resume завершает работу до ADB/device-команды; повреждённый файл остаётся байт-в-байт неизменным, прежние записи журнала сохраняются и добавляется одна запись `FAILED`.
5. Корректные identity + невалидный отдельный per-run JSON: не запускать недостающие cases до обнаружения corruption; сохранить исходный файл и прежние записи журнала, добавив одну запись `FAILED`.
6. Контроли: device/APK mismatch и corrupted per-run evidence остаются fail-closed; валидный cycle по-прежнему resume/aggregate/validate/package проходить может.
7. Аналоги: перед выводом разобрать `build_s1_aggregate_from_disk` и `cmd_package` fallback с raw logs. Ни одна ошибка, влияющая на обязательный вход принятия, не должна пропасть между runner и завершающим валидатором.

**Условие принятия этого инженерного среза:** regression evidence для сценариев 1–6 на точном harness commit показывает: invalid attempt journal даёт nonzero exit до device/case operation и остаётся байт-в-байт неизменным; invalid cycle JSON даёт nonzero exit до device/case operation, сам файл остаётся неизменным, существующие attempt records сохраняются как prefix и добавляется ровно одна запись `FAILED`; valid control сохраняет прежние attempts и проходит существующие aggregate/package validation checks. Полный host suite запускается один раз на передаваемом commit. Продуктовые gate/task/CAP статусы не меняются. Это не закрывает B1/B2: дополнительно обязательны полный field/file/command inventory, reviewer и новый DEV run по §7.

## 1.2 Candidate audit — S1 instrumentation completion result (2026-09-28)

Scope: Gate B1/B2, I11/I21/I22, FROZEN Spike Protocol v1.1 §3.5 steps 5–11.
Source baseline `31375c63`, `host/runner.py` v1.6, SHA-256
`e527d6248190235b2b515f9609d8ef254be57a6ff717c0a2308789d9ea265bf4`; product
remains `.216` / code 335. This was initially a source-derived finding. Its
corrected Android result interpretation is in plan revision 5.360; implementation
and current verification are recorded in plan revision 5.361 and the checksummed
bundle `execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/`.

**User result:** after an injected process death, S1 must observe a successful
read-only snapshot and successful aggregate before the host can accept the run.
FROZEN §3.5 requires restarting the app, capturing the snapshot and verifying the
oracle. ADB/instrumentation failure cannot substitute for that result. The
`start_case` instrumentation command is a distinct intentional-kill operation:
its nonzero result may be expected only when the run-specific `KILL_INJECT` marker,
PID termination check and subsequent snapshot/evidence checks all succeed.

**Class-wide command-result inventory in `host/runner.py`:**

| Site | Command / completion signal | Source audit | Disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `_execute_single_s1_case`, `res_snap` (~441–443) | `am instrument ... action snapshot`; accepts stdout containing `OK (` OR `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1`, while omitting `returncode` | Snapshot action does not intentionally kill its instrumentation process (`S1Harness.runStep`, `S1Harness.java` snapshot branch). Android `-1` is `Activity.RESULT_OK`, not failure; accepting that marker alone can count runner-level success when the expected test did not run. A nonzero adb exit plus success-like text is also unexamined. | **Finding:** require adb return code 0 and exactly `OK (1 test)`; if a final instrumentation code is present it must be `-1`; reject failure/abort markers. Allow `-1` with the expected test summary. |
| `_run_s1_host_orchestrated`, `res_agg` (~644–647) | `am instrument ... action aggregate`; accepts `OK (` OR `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1`, while omitting `returncode` | Aggregate action does not intentionally kill its instrumentation process; the same runner-level marker and adb-status gap applies. | **Same finding:** require adb return code 0 and exactly `OK (1 test)`; if a final instrumentation code is present it must be `-1`; reject failure/abort markers. Allow `-1` with the expected test summary. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case`, `res_case` (~394–418) | `start_case` intentionally kills the app process at the named hook | Not a success-text-only path: requires matching barrier + run_id + PID, checks `/proc/<pid>` is gone, then requires snapshot and pulled evidence | Protected special case; preserve the intended process-death behavior |
| `_execute_single_s1_case`, `proc_check` and retry (~423–434) | shell checks `/proc/<pid>` | Return code is checked and only exact `PROCESS_GONE` is accepted | Protected |
| `_run_instrumented` (~275–281), `cmd_run_all` S2–S6 (~733–737) | S2–S6 instrumentation | Both check nonzero exit, `OK (` and absence of `FAILURES!!!` | Protected analogs; retain regression coverage |
| `cmd_audit`, `measure_device_metadata`, resume identity/hash queries, required pulls | ADB state/metadata/hash/file reads | Return code and/or exact output, existence, length or later checksum/schema validation is required | Protected by source checks; broader field-level mapping remains open |
| `check_file` (~446–448), `cmd_package` raw-log copy (~2920–2930), optional device-log pull (~484–490, 654) | Success marker / best-effort copies | Per-run required evidence is still pulled and `raw_log_location` is checked against packaged bytes; the optional device-log copy is not the authoritative S1 raw host transcript | Not the same false-success path; include in remaining I07/I21/I22 inventory |
| `logcat -c`, failure-path logcat capture, force-stop, pre-run `rm -rf`, extra `logcat_ALL` capture, PM permission grants | Reset/hygiene/diagnostics | Distinct from successful snapshot/aggregate; each has downstream run-identity, process, required-evidence or instrumentation checks, or is supplemental telemetry | Keep recorded as command analogs; do not infer these establish a complete I21 inventory |

The two candidate false-acceptance sites are the only S1 normal snapshot/aggregate
completion paths in this class that accept a runner-level `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE`
marker without requiring the targeted test result and without checking adb's
return code. Android defines `Activity.RESULT_OK` as `-1`; Android's test-result
parser also treats final `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1` as instrumentation success,
including when no tests ran. Therefore `-1` must remain allowed with the expected
one-test summary, and must not substitute for that summary. See [Android
Activity.RESULT_OK](https://developer.android.com/reference/android/app/Activity#RESULT_OK)
and the [Android instrumentation result parser tests](https://android.googlesource.com/platform/tools/base/+/088f74c4fa52d08871b31635be96ab8ae05c87bb%5E!/).
The intentional `start_case` call is excluded: it kills the app process by design,
and its acceptance remains based on the run-specific marker, PID, observed process
death, then valid snapshot/evidence. This list does not close the wider I21
inventory across commands, fields or S2–S6.

**Required positive/negative controls before the fix:**

1. Positive S1 run/snapshot and aggregate controls still complete using normal
   zero-exit `OK` output and preserve existing evidence.
2. Snapshot returns nonzero with `OK (1 test)`; runner exits before treating the
   per-run JSON/evidence as a successful snapshot.
3. Snapshot returns zero with only `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1` and no one-test summary; runner fails closed. With `OK (1 test)` plus `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1`, the runner accepts the successful Android result.
4. Aggregate returns nonzero with `OK (1 test)`; runner fails before final success.
5. Aggregate returns zero with only `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1` and no one-test summary; runner fails closed. With `OK (1 test)` plus `INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1`, the runner accepts the successful Android result.
6. `OK (0 tests)`, any final instrumentation result code other than `-1`, `INSTRUMENTATION_FAILED`, or `INSTRUMENTATION_ABORTED` never counts as the expected test. `OK (1 test)` with nonzero adb return code also fails closed.
7. Intentional `start_case` process death remains accepted only with its unique
   marker, PID termination, successful snapshot and valid evidence; unrelated
   S2–S6 tests continue checking nonzero exit.

**Acceptance condition:** addressed negative tests fail at the corresponding
snapshot/aggregate boundary and prevent later success-dependent commands; positive
controls accept `OK (1 test)` with zero adb status and optional
`INSTRUMENTATION_CODE: -1`; the intentional process-death control remains valid.
Focused orchestrator tests and one complete host/build/traceability/task-ledger/
evidence check pass on the final source; raw logs/checksums are stored. Increment
runner version 1.6 → 1.7. No app version, frozen threshold, gate, task or CAP
status changes. B1/B2 still require the complete I01–I25 field/file/command
inventory, independent reviewer and a new DEV run under §7.

**Engineering result (2026-09-28):** runner v1.7 commit `fa4d7ad2`, SHA-256
`95b75edd16df18fc7ef939a79dc8202c319ae70d84d7fbd44ccc5162f97dcbab`; focused
controls 3/3, orchestrator suite 22/22, configured `check_all.sh --rerun` exit 0
(Gradle 123 tasks; host 107/107; traceability/task ledger/non-strict B0 evidence
validation passed). Exact outputs and checksums are under
`execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/`. No device was
connected: instrumentation and DEV were NOT RUN. This closes the host-only
engineering slice only; B1/B2 remain IN_PROGRESS and REVIEW_REQUIRED.

## 1. Обязательный порядок ревью

1. До ревью нового runner обновить этот документ по frozen-протоколу, схемам,
   producer-коду Android, host-коду и реальным пакетам. Diff — только указатель изменений.
2. Для каждого поля, вложенного поля, файла и внешней операции проверить:
   **P** Presence, **F** Format, **C** Cross-check, **E** Failure propagation.
3. Найденный дефект классифицировать по P/F/C/E. Выполнить поиск того же паттерна
   по ВСЕМУ `host/runner.py`, затем по связанным validator/producer-контрактам.
   В одном отчёте перечислить все найденные места, включая защищённые аналоги.
4. До реализации фикса передать Gravity полный набор негативных случаев для
   затронутого класса инвариантов. Фикс должен покрывать класс, а не одну строку.
5. Принимать по исходникам, исполненным регрессионным тестам и новому DEV-циклу.
   Не считать число зелёных тестов доказательством полноты.

`if x:`, `.get(..., 0)`, `except`, `check=False` сами по себе не дефекты.
Для каждого места необходимо доказать обязательность значения, разрешённый fallback
или обязательную последующую обработку ошибки. Поиск — инвентаризация рисков,
не автоматическое доказательство обхода.

## 2. Формат полного реестра

Каждая строка итогового реестра соответствует ОДНОМУ конкретному JSON path,
файловому контракту либо внешней операции. Группы ниже — начальная карта scope,
НЕ замена поэлементной инвентаризации и НЕ основание заявить 100% покрытия.

Обязательные колонки для развёрнутых строк:

`ID | source/section | artifact + JSON path | spike/mode/condition | P | F | C | E | code commit:path:line/symbol | test ID for each property | execution/evidence | reviewer verdict`

Для P/F/C/E допустимы `TODO`, `PASS`, `FAIL`, `N/A:обоснование`.
PASS требует проверенного кода и исполненного теста. Для условного поля явно
описать условие обязательности и протестировать обе ветви. Nullable telemetry
требует причины unavailable/censored и не может подменять наблюдение нулём.
Копии одного утверждения в двух JSON не являются независимым измерением.

## 3. Начальная карта инвариантов и угроз

Все строки ниже имеют P/F/C/E = TODO; ссылки — места проверки, не вердикт.
Списки полей в строках обязательно развернуть перед закрытием соответствующего gate.

| ID | Поля/объекты и scope | Требуемый контракт P/F/C/E | Негативные тесты до фикса |
|---|---|---|---|
| I01 | Все JSON, корень, schema/manifest version, классификация файла | Обязательный файл; допустимый тип корня и версия; однозначная роль; parse/read failure исключает PASS | absent, empty, truncated, list вместо object, unsupported version, unknown role |
| I02 | `spike_id`, `case_id`, `injection_hook`, QA linkage | Непустые enum; точное соответствие нормативному registry и сценарию | missing/null/blank/wrong type, unknown spike/case/hook, допустимый префикс с выдуманным суффиксом |
| I03 | `run_id`, `operation_id`, cycle/attempt identity | Валидные уникальные IDs, связь с fixture и командой; дубликаты не увеличивают completeness | duplicate, ID чужого run, повтор файла под другим именем, wrong operation |
| I04 | `execution_mode`, `dut_id`, `device_serial`/`hardware_serial`, fingerprint, `app_build_hash` | Явные типы/форматы; согласованная identity всех артефактов и измеренного устройства/APK; отсутствие источника — отказ | null/пустой/string-null/hash wrong format, mixed modes/APKs/serials, emulator QUAL, перестановка порядка файлов |
| I05 | Device manufacturer/model/sku/OS/API/security patch/kernel/filesystem/ABI/MemTotal | Условная обязательность по schema; без выдуманных defaults; независимые измерения | getprop/df/hash command error, empty output, malformed number, wrong path filesystem, heap вместо RAM |
| I06 | `start_monotonic_ns`, `end_monotonic_ns`, dump timestamps, latency fields | Конечные числа, не bool/NaN/Inf; порядок, единицы и происхождение; unavailable явно | zero, reversed, wrong units, fabricated fallback, timestamp чужого запуска |
| I07 | `raw_log_location`, raw evidence paths, logs/transcripts | Непустой файл внутри package; связь run/operation/hook/PID; host-log не перезаписывается device-log | null, empty log, directory instead of file, absolute/traversal/escaping symlink, foreign log, overwritten transcript |
| I08 | Dump identity, `dump_type`, mode/time/device/hash | Оба требуемых dump; полный типизированный объект; filename/run/type/identity связаны с per-run | missing directory/file, swapped pre/post, foreign run, missing comparison operand |
| I09 | Dump published/temp exists/length/hash, ArtifactRecord, references, owners, workflow state | Полная схема снимка и идентичность объектов; external и embedded snapshots согласованы; read error не равен пустому состоянию | отсутствующее поле с обеих сторон, corrupt bytes, foreign owner/ref, diagnostic exception, partial transaction |
| I10 | `.bin` и ожидаемые/наблюдаемые length/SHA-256 | Нормативная условная обязательность по D-hook; тип/формат; host bytes против независимого fixture oracle | missing/zero/truncated/modified bin, null expected/observed hash, wrong size/type, запрещённая publication |
| I11 | `outcome`, `kill_condition_triggered`, `artifact_checks[*].check/passed`, diagnostic flags | Закрытая схема; все обязательные проверки присутствуют; нарушения распространяются в aggregate и exit status | absent outcome/check, bool/string confusion, unknown violation representation, per-run FAIL + aggregate PASS |
| I12 | Все aggregate counters, findings, totals, quantiles | Обязательные неотрицательные integer counters (не bool); пересчёт из attempts/findings/raw events; missing не равен 0 | omit/null/string/negative/fractional counter, mismatch totals, erased findings, fabricated percentiles |
| I13 | Expected/observed case matrices, repeats, workload/fixture/point registry | Точная матрица из зафиксированного mode/profile; каждый слот уникален, неполнота запрещает gate PASS | aggregate-only, one run per category, extra/duplicate/missing slot, changed profile, subset relabelled PASS |
| I14 | S2 C1–C7, visible Page, timestamps, dimensions/JPEG bytes, StateStore queue | Реальные события и данные; Page commit/display коррелируют; метрики измеряют заявленное | fake C7, no draw/hidden/wrong Page, queue=in-flight, missing JPEG, false committed count |
| I15 | S2 W0/W1/W2 permit owner/history, background results, burst interval, capture wait | Реальная нагрузка/admission до burst и на нужном интервале; нет подавленных ошибок | signal before admission, gaps, adapter denied/failed, fake timestamps, no counter producer |
| I16 | S3 envelope/profile, languages/formats, tokens/admission, PSS/RSS, candidate commits | DEV input не product approval; actual live buffers и limits; persistent conditional commit и реальные измерения | undefined inputs marked defined, RSS=privateDirty, stale local constants, timeout releases live OCR, unaccounted allocation |
| I17 | S4 `P_MAX`, render envelope, `pdf_artifact_path`, SaveManifest ID/page_count/page_ids/page_hashes/payload hash | Полный manifest и frozen order; нормативные points; каждый ожидаемый PDF существует и связан с run | missing PDF/manifest field/hash, duplicate page ID, changed manifest, reorder/substitute PDF |
| I18 | PDF pages, image bytes, rendering/content evidence, validator version/results | Структура + реальные pixels против эталона; нельзя принять лишь неиспользуемый ресурс/надпись | blank PDF, unused/off-page/covered raster, background-colour cover, missing details, malformed stream, renderer failure |
| I19 | Health ANR/OOM/observation_failed/error и memory counters S2–S4 | Scope по процессу/времени; дедупликация; unknown не значит healthy; failure не подавляется | stale/unrelated event, low-memory вместо OOM, permission/API error, null observer, missing flag |
| I20 | S5/S6 все поля, hooks, providers, recovery dumps | Развернуть из frozen-протокола до реализации/приёмки; real provider и отдельный oracle каждого R-case | missing E/R case, provider mock passed as real, missing dump, non-idempotent recovery, R31 misclassification |
| I21 | Commands/ADB start, kill, snapshot, pull, hash, instrumentation, aggregate | Ограниченный timeout; typed expected process death; проверка returncode и результата; ошибка не заменяет kill | hung command, nonzero with OK text, marker without death, wrong PID, failed/empty pull, stdout/stderr loss |
| I22 | Cleanup, failure capture, raw log harvest | Уже полученные evidence сохраняются до cleanup; ошибки не превращаются в успешную чистую среду | disconnect, failed cleanup, interrupted loop, log read failure, aggregate failure, lost prior attempts |
| I23 | `resume_attempts[*]`: timestamp/spike/mode/serial/outcome/executed_runs/error | Валидный журнал; все identity источники проверены до действий; attempts сохраняются; SUCCESS после validation | missing identity, mixed tuple, malformed attempts, corrupt JSON deletion, resume twice duplicates, unsupported spike |
| I24 | Package manifest, checksums, links, relocated raw artifacts | Самодостаточный package; повторная проверка после копирования; не зависит от source dir | relocate/remove source, broken refs, corrupt packaged byte, omitted attempts, wrong manifest hash |
| I25 | `tasks.json`, deps, source IDs, commit/evidence, gate verdict | DONE требует покрытия и принятого evidence; status string не доказательство | DONE without proof, unresolved dependency, stale hash, missing test, IN_PROGRESS treated complete |

## 4. Первый сквозной поиск по runner.py

Срез `e5eede6`; ниже все перечисленные места подлежат проверке и привязке к
строкам полного реестра. Это кандидаты на аудит, не список уже воспроизведённых багов.

| Класс паттерна | Места в runner.py (строки исходного среза) | Обязательное обобщение |
|---|---|---|
| Optional comparison operands | manifest 763–771; dumps 805–813; per-run 874–883; aggregate 1105–1114; disk aggregation 1594,1611 | Проверить обязательность обоих fingerprint/hash/expected значений во всех режимах/артефактах |
| Missing counter becomes zero | 1130–1140; 1304–1307; 1335–1347; 1359–1362; 1416–1422 | Описать каждое поле, где default допустим, и запретить default для обязательного наблюдения |
| Optional cross-check/schema | embedded dumps 1010,1013; per-run outcome/checks 1148–1164 | Обязательные поля должны проверяться даже когда удалены с обеих сторон; checks нельзя отключить пустым списком |
| Missing observation flag | 1252,1330 | Проверять наличие и строгий bool, а не только равенство True |
| Swallowed exception/fallback | 265; metadata/aggregate 1538; identity scan 1794; attempts 1859 | Различить допустимое чтение альтернативы и потерю обязательного evidence; журнал не сбрасывать молча |
| Unchecked external failures | instrumentation 208; logs 239,298,326,380,661; metadata 426–445; cleanup 471–477; final pulls 570 | Просмотреть ВСЕ check=False, включая защищённые 352–358,415–417; документировать последующую проверку каждого |
| Truthiness вместо type/format | serial aliases 772,814,884,1115; outcome 847,1075; P_MAX 1356; PDF aliases 1395 | Общие schema validators, конфликтующие aliases отклонять; zero/null отдельно от falsy |

Повторяемый поиск (после каждого исправления дополнять новыми паттернами):

```sh
rg -n 'check=False|except|\.get\(|if .*sha|if .*serial|if .*hash|if .*fp' host/runner.py
rg -n 'or 0|, 0\)|is True|if .* in .*dump|return.*PASS' host/runner.py
```

## 5. Матрица adversarial-тестов для Gravity — до реализации

Для КАЖДОГО обязательного поля из полного реестра параметризовать:

- **T-P:** удалить; null; пустая строка/whitespace; пустой контейнер; строка `null`.
- **T-F:** неправильный scalar/container, bool вместо number, NaN/Infinity,
  отрицательное/дробное значение счётчика, unknown enum, повреждённый формат hash.
- **T-C:** заменить только один источник; оба JSON согласованы, но диск/fixture
  отличается; foreign run/operation/device; duplicate ID; переставить порядок файлов.
- **T-E:** сбой чтения/hash/renderer/ADB; timeout; nonzero с правдоподобным stdout;
  отсутствие файла при returncode=0; ожидаемая остановка до зависимого шага.
- **T-ROUNDTRIP:** полный положительный fixture, мутация, package/relocate/validate,
  resume и повторный resume. Обязателен контроль отсутствия побочных действий после отказа.

Для optional/условных полей тестировать допустимое отсутствие и его границу.
Невалидный пакет может возвращать validation error, наблюдённое нарушение — FAIL,
отсутствие доказательства — INCOMPLETE; ни одно из них не даёт успешную приёмку gate.
Успешная проверка формата FAIL/INCOMPLETE-пакета не равна успешному experimental outcome.
Каждый тест должен ломать именно целевой инвариант на иначе корректном fixture;
отказ по другой причине не подтверждает защиту. Mock не должен подменять проверяемый код.

## 6. Формулировка следующего requests-to-fix

> Валидатор нигде не должен принимать PASS при отсутствии обязательного поля
> сравнения, обязательного файла или результата наблюдения. Инвентаризировать
> каждый такой operand в runner.py и связанных валидаторах. Для каждого указать
> P/F/C/E, code location и negative test. Найденный класс распространить на все
> применимые места в одном согласованном исправлении; исключения обосновать.
> Не менять frozen outcomes, не ослаблять tests, не объявлять неизвестное нулём.

Форма отчёта одной находки:
`ID → P/F/C/E → воспроизведение → ВСЕ аналогичные места → исключения → tests → fix/evidence → verdict`.

## 7. Условия закрытия B1/B2

- Полный field/file/command inventory выведен из нормативных источников И producer/schema/code.
- Включены вложенные paths и conditional поля; все группы разделены на отдельные строки.
- Нет полей без классификации и нет TODO. Для N/A есть согласованное обоснование.
- Для каждой применимой P/F/C/E проверки есть code reference и исполненный regression test.
- Проведён сквозной поиск аналогичных паттернов; ни один найденный участок не оставлен без решения.
- Новый DEV-прогон соответствует проверенному APK/code commit; raw evidence и package валидны.
- Независимый reviewer подтвердил матрицу и результат. Самооценка Gravity не заменяет review.

Покрытие считать отдельно: inventory completeness, tests mapped, tests executed,
reviewed PASS. Знаменатель — полный подтверждённый inventory; до его завершения
процент покрытия **не определён**, не 100%. S5/S6 не исключать молча из общего B1.

На момент создания документа B1/B2 остаются **IN_PROGRESS по новой методологии**.
Исторические DEV-пакеты и локальные приёмки сохраняются; новые дефекты ими не
предполагаются. Это изменение критерия полной приёмки, а не отмена измерений.

frozen aggregate templates and comparing exact paths with this sample found:

All other protocol result-template paths were present in the current aggregate sample, including every frozen S1–S4 path. The sample also contains implementation/diagnostic additions (for example, `qualification_outcome`, `validation_note`, S2 workload timing/health fields, and S6 duplicated `device_metadata`); no frozen unknown-field policy or mode mapping has yet been located. These are inventory findings, not a ruling that every addition is invalid.

The base `validate_dir` required per-run list currently includes `spike_id`,
`dut_id`, `run_id`, `case_id`, `operation_id`, `device_build_fingerprint`,
`app_build_hash`, `start_monotonic_ns`, `end_monotonic_ns`,
`measured_metrics`, `artifact_checks`, `raw_log_location`, and
`execution_mode`; S1 adds `injection_hook`, `pre_state_dump`, and
`post_state_dump`. QUALIFICATION mode separately requires a device serial.
Protocol §9 additionally names `injection_hook`, `pre_state_dump`,
`post_state_dump`, `kill_condition_triggered`, and `notes` as common keys.
Negative tests for each of those omissions are not yet mapped across all
spikes/modes. A per-run pass for one spike does not close the analogous path in
another spike.

**Current disposition:** partial source-to-shape comparison only. The rows above
are not independent review verdicts. I01–I25 path catalogue, exact P/F/C/E and
test mapping, the full command/cleanup inventory, resume/package/gate path maps,
and mode-condition classification remain open. Per the active package contract, runner code changes wait until the exact map and
consolidated batch contract are complete; existing evidence remains intact.

### 11.1 Exact template totals and observed aggregate differences

v1.1, then compared recursively with the corresponding `dev-results/*.json`
sample. The frozen aggregate templates contain 263 concrete/wildcard paths:
S1=39, S2=68, S3=40, S4=33, S5=47, S6=36. The common per-run template in §9
has 17 top-level paths before expanding conditional nested dump, metric, and
check objects. The sample has 272 JSON files (2 manifests, 6 aggregates, 216
per-run records, 48 dumps); it is a DEV shape sample only.

### 11.2 External command and file-operation inventory — source pass

An AST walk of the tested `host/runner.py` v1.7 source (`fa4d7ad2`) found 48
`run_cmd` call sites: 31 use `check=False`, 3 explicitly use `check=True`, and
14 inherit `run_cmd`'s strict default. All run through its 60-second timeout;
timeout is represented as exit 124 (strict callers exit; non-strict callers
must inspect the result). This table records all non-strict call-site groups;
the three strict final S1 pulls and strict-default calls are protected by
`run_cmd` exit handling, but downstream file/schema checks still apply.

| Call site(s) in `host/runner.py` | Operation / consumer | Existing guard or evidence | Inventory finding / required disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cmd_audit:203` | `adb get-state` | Checks return code and exact `device` output. | Protected for the state assertion. No command mutation test is mapped. |
| `cmd_install:264–265` | Grant CAMERA to app and test APK | APK installs are strict; grants are not inspected; command prints installed afterward. | Result is not independently verified. Map `install` result vs later instrumentation and define failure propagation as part of command batch. |
| `_run_instrumented:277` | Single S2–S6 instrumentation invocation | Checks rc, presence of substring `OK (`, and absence of `FAILURES!!!`. | **Source-level false-acceptance candidate:** `OK (0 tests)` and a wrong nonzero test count satisfy the textual predicate. No mutation regression mapped. |
| `_run_instrumented:308` | Logcat after instrumentation | Explicitly checks return code before writing log. | Protected for this capture. `run_cmd` error and timeout tests cover the generic wrapper, not the capture callsite. |
| `record_failure_and_exit:394` | Best-effort diagnostic logcat when an S1 case fails | Its stdout is included in failure log; return code/stderr are ignored. | Diagnostic-only fallback; logcat failure can leave empty diagnostics. Must remain a failed run and be recorded distinctly; no injected capture-failure test mapped. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:415` | Clear logcat before a new run | Return code ignored. | Precondition result is not proven. Include cleanup/clear failure and stale-log isolation in batch. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:419` | `start_case` instrumentation intentionally kills app | Its rc is not the oracle; exact run-specific `KILL_INJECT` marker, PID and `/proc` death are required. | Intentional exception; preserve. Existing process-death and host-orchestration controls cover portions; marker/PID collision and command-output mutation remain to map. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:422` | Read logcat for `KILL_INJECT` | Return code ignored; stdout searched for exact run marker/PID; can also use instrumentation stdout. | Marker/process-death protects false kill success; logcat read failure is not separately surfaced. Map stdout-vs-logcat source and failure case. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:448,454` | Check `/proc/<pid>` after kill, then one retry | Requires rc=0 and exact `PROCESS_GONE`; retry still existing means fail. | Protected. `test_run_case_enforces_process_death_check` and `test_resume_enforces_process_death_check` are reusable; exact S1 path mapping remains. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:462` | Force-stop after verified death | Return code ignored. | Hygiene only after death observation. Record as non-gating cleanup, but do not let failure authorize reuse of stale evidence. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:466,672` | Snapshot and aggregate instrumentation | `_s1_instrumentation_succeeded`: rc 0, exactly `OK (1 test)`, no failure/abort and valid optional final code. | Protected by `test_s1_snapshot_rejects_false_or_incomplete_success`, `test_s1_aggregate_rejects_false_or_incomplete_success`, and exact-source report; preserve this boundary. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:474` | Check per-run JSON presence on device | Looks for `OK` in stdout from `test -f … && echo OK || echo MISSING`; return code not separately checked. | Positive/error output relationship has no dedicated regression. Mandatory subsequent pulls and `validate_dir` are further guards. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:479` | Capture successful-run logcat | Return code ignored; stdout copied into host transcript. | Core run is later validated, but transcript can silently be empty/incomplete. Map this diagnostic evidence failure. |
| `_execute_single_s1_case:515` | Pull per-run JSON, pre/post dumps, conditional S1 binaries and optional device log | Required pulls check rc, local existence and non-zero size; device log is optional. | Required pull controls exist (`test_mandatory_pull_*`); optional device-log failure is not distinguished from absence. Keep optionality conditional and explicit. |
| `adb_text:526` | Device metadata shell command wrapper | Checks rc and nonempty stdout; parses API, MemTotal and `df -T /data`; callers cover manufacturer/model/SKU/release/API/fingerprint/security patch/ABI/kernel. | Protected wrapper path. No focused command-mutation tests mapped for malformed `df`, MemTotal or field-specific shell error. |
| `_run_s1_host_orchestrated:583–589` | Remove old device evidence/timing/artifact roots; force-stop; clear logcat before a cycle | All seven results ignored; subsequent loop requires fresh run marker/process death and unique run IDs. | **Unclassified cleanup failure class:** stale state may remain after failed cleanup. Need positive control plus each cleanup class failing without a falsely accepted cycle or overwriting earlier host evidence. |
| `_run_s1_host_orchestrated:682–684` | Final pull of S1 run evidence, dumps and artifacts | Strict `check=True`; validator verifies per-run contents, dumps and conditional artifact files. | Protected; `test_final_pull_subdirectories_do_not_overwrite_host_logs` and mandatory-pull regressions apply. |
| `_run_s1_host_orchestrated:685` | Pull optional device log directory | Return code ignored; not used as mandatory per-run evidence. | Optional field; define/document when absent vs failed, and ensure no manifest claims it was captured. |
| `cmd_run_all:764` | S2–S6 instrumentation loop (five suite invocations) | Same `OK (` substring test as single-spike runner. | Same false-acceptance candidate as line 277, repeated over the whole package. One generalized regression must cover every S2–S6 target. |
| `cmd_run_all:778` | Full logcat for the all-spike cycle | Return code ignored; stdout is written as `logcat_ALL.log`; validation does not require it. | Diagnostic evidence may be silently incomplete. Map it against I07/I22 and package claim; not a product-spike outcome oracle. |
| `cmd_resume:2735` | Read current device build fingerprint | Explicitly checks rc and nonempty stdout. | Protected; resume fingerprint missing/mismatch tests already exist. |
| `cmd_resume:2741` | `pm path` for installed app | rc ignored; requires at least one output line containing `package:`. | **Result inconsistency candidate:** nonzero command with success-like package stdout could proceed to APK hash query. Add rc+stdout mutation control. |
| `cmd_resume:2748` | Device APK `sha256sum` | Checks rc and nonempty stdout, takes first token, then compares with cycle hash. | Mismatch/missing tests exist; malformed digest and plausible stdout on nonzero result not mapped. |

The remaining strict/default `run_cmd` sites are also inventoried: APK build
(`cmd_build:237`), app/test installs (`cmd_install:262–263`), start cleanup,
logcat clear and evidence pull (`_run_instrumented:270,273,316`), device
property reads (`cmd_audit:209–214`), and cycle evidence/log pulls
(`cmd_run_all:772,777`). APK existence/hash operations at `cmd_build:242–247`
are host filesystem checks, not `run_cmd` sites. Strict/default subprocesses
use generic runner exit handling; property emptiness and local file-presence
conditions are checked separately and are not inferred from rc=0.

The source walk also identified these host filesystem boundaries in the same
finite set: JSON/hash reads of `configs/frozen_hashes.json`, manifests, every
cycle JSON, `resume_attempts.json`, and gate ledgers; writing cycle manifests,
host/device transcripts, resume journals, aggregate JSON, package manifest,
report and checksum file; per-run evidence and final subtree pulls; raw/log/dump/
artifact copy into package; package-wide SHA-256 generation/verification;
and cleanup with `shutil.rmtree(target_dir)` in `cmd_package`. `_safe_package_path`
is used for per-run log references and checks traversal/symlink escape. Resume
journal corruption is preserved and rejected by `_load_resume_attempts`; its
existing corruption/malformed-history tests are reusable.

Source-level package findings against `docs/05_RESULTS_PACKAGE_CONTRACT.md`:

1. `cmd_package` constructs `spikes_included` as all six S1–S6 regardless of
   whether each aggregate was found/copied. `validate_dir` classifies and
   validates aggregates that exist but has no all-six set assertion. The frozen
   package contract requires all six aggregates. Existing checksum round-trip
   positive fixture intentionally contains only a DEV S1 declaration; preserve
   that standalone/subset validation use and put the all-six rule on final
   qualification-package creation/validation only.
2. `qualification-manifest.json` currently contains only `manifest_version`,
   `execution_mode`, `dut_id`, `app_package`, `generated_at`, `source_cycle`,
   `hard_power_extension_executed`, `frozen_hashes_verified`, and
   `spikes_included`. Contract 05 additionally requires exact manufacturer,
   model, SKU, Android/API/fingerprint/security patch/kernel/filesystem/ABI/
   MemTotal, app version/build hash, host runner revision/hash and execution
   start/end. Those exact manifest paths have no producer, validator or mutation
   test mapping yet. Do not synthesize physical fields from a DEV emulator.
3. `frozen_hashes_verified` is written as a Boolean result, but `cmd_package`
   does not stop if it is false and `validate_dir` does not enforce it. `verify-gates`
   independently checks hashes; map which final-package entry point is required
   to reject mismatch before assigning the final verdict.
4. `cmd_package` deletes an existing target with `shutil.rmtree` before copying
   or validating, and does not reject a source/target alias. A copy, hash or
   validation failure can therefore destroy the previously usable package; a
   source-target alias can erase the source tree. This is a source-level
   destructive-boundary finding; no command was run against output paths and no
   data loss was reproduced. The package contract does not state overwrite
   semantics; retain the currently delivered package until staged output has
   passed validation, then replace atomically, and reject aliases.
5. `cmd_verify_gates` uses `.get("gates", {})` and `.get("tasks", [])` and can
   iterate empty collections; the on-disk `execution/tasks.json` has 12 gates
   and 11 tasks, while `execution/product_tasks.json` is the separate product
   ledger. The route must remain explicit about which ledger is normative for
   this harness subcommand. No mutation test for missing/wrong/empty container
   shape is mapped. This candidate concerns schema rejection; it does not
   authorize status changes.
6. During `cmd_package` raw-log repair, `raw_log_location` is joined to both
   source and target without `_safe_package_path`; `mkdir`/`copy2` may therefore
   touch a path outside the package before the later `validate_dir` rejects the
   reference. This is a source-level path-boundary candidate only: it has not
   been executed or reproduced. Add traversal, absolute-path, and symlink
   controls that assert no outside-tree mutation and preserve the input cycle.

### 11.3 Consolidated runner batch — scope fixed by revision 5.391

These source-derived candidates converge on one user result: S1–S6 harness
evidence and its delivery package must not be reported as more complete than
the commands and files actually prove. The list is finite for the audited
runner v1.7 source. Revision 5.391 completes the I01–I25 mapping and classifies
each candidate against DEV and QUALIFICATION conditions; the implementation
batch is now ready under the unchanged active-package contract. Exact targets,
evidence bounds and residual actions are in §11.19.

| Batch slice | Required positive and negative controls | Required proof artifact / exit condition |
|---|---|---|
| Instrumentation completion (`_run_instrumented`, `cmd_run_all`) | Correct one-test summary; zero tests; wrong/multiple count; malformed summary; nonzero rc with success text; failure/abort marker despite success-like text. Parameterize over S2–S6 targets and preserve S1's exact parser/intentional-kill exception. | Host regression output proving only the exact expected completion passes, every negative stops before evidence pull/package, and existing S1 controls remain intact. |
| Exact spike matrix and package inclusion | Complete six-aggregate package positive; each S1–S6 aggregate missing in turn; manifest list missing/extra/duplicate or disagrees with copied files; per-spike count above and below expected DEV/QUALIFICATION matrix; control: standalone single-spike validation remains supported. | Mutation report listing each S1–S6 omission/overcount and proving no final package claims a missing result; valid six-spike package round-trip passes. |
| Frozen package manifest and hash claims | Delete/null/wrong-type each field mandated by Contract 05; conflict device/app/runner tuple or start/end; make frozen hash mismatch; preserve explicit optional hard-power false case. | Field-level mutation output and exact manifest schema tied to the physical run tuple; invalid/mismatched package fails closed without creating a valid final verdict. |
| Frozen aggregate schema and measurement claims | For each exact path in §11.10, mutate missing/null/wrong type/invalid enum; reconcile counters, quantiles, point metrics and run matrices with independent per-run/raw evidence. Exercise S2 percentiles, S3 envelope/version/resource fields, S4 validator/raster/SaveManifest paths, conditional S5 provider tuples and S6 startup/table paths. Preserve justified DEV-only/optional conditions and standalone single-spike validation. | One path-indexed mutation report shows every required row rejects omission/contradiction and each conditional absence remains valid only in its documented branch; one complete six-spike positive package passes. |
| Package filesystem transaction | source=target, target inside source, incomplete input, injected copy failure, checksum failure and validation failure with an existing valid destination; positive staged-package validation and replacement. | Evidence the source and old destination survive each negative; only a fully validated staged tree becomes the destination; checksums validate after relocation. |
| `check=False` command-result propagation | `pm grant`, `pm path`, device property/metadata, logcat capture, each cleanup category, optional device-log pull and per-run presence check: rc nonzero with plausible stdout, empty/malformed output, timeout, transport failure. Preserve commands intentionally treated as optional/expected-kill only where an independent oracle protects them. | Per-command expectation matrix with no false success, exact operation halted/continued according to contract, diagnostics preserved, and no stale run accepted. |
| Gate ledger schema | Missing, empty, wrong-type `gates` / `tasks`; valid positive with one complete gate/task; incomplete status/dependency/evidence. Strict and non-strict modes. | Neither mode reports all gates complete for malformed/empty input; strict exits nonzero; non-strict lists unresolved state. No ledger statuses change. |
| Package log-reference repair | `raw_log_location` values using `../`, an absolute path, and a symlink escape; source has a corresponding nonempty file; valid in-package missing-log repair control. | Invalid references cause no writes outside staged package; valid repair remains within package and final references still validate. Preserve source and prior destination on all failures. |

The inventory and test contract are complete at revision 5.391; execution is
still NOT RUN for the newly listed cells. Existing checks and evidence above
are retained and must not be rerun merely to refresh counts. The package is
accepted only by the evidence condition in §11.19, not by this document update.

### 11.5 S1 standalone dump and binary paths

Normative sources: Protocol §§3.5, 3.7, 3.9; producer `S1Diagnostic.Snapshot.toJson`
and `S1Database.readArtifactRecord/readArtifactReferences/readAuthoritativePages`;
consumers `validate_dir` and `build_s1_aggregate_from_disk`. There are 48 DEV
dump files (three repeats × eight barriers × pre/post); observations below are

#### 11.5.2 S1 read-only state oracle and cross-file binding — revision 5.381

Protocol §3.5 requires the host to inspect pre-recovery bytes, ArtifactRecord,
every ArtifactReference, and the owning entity. The finite barrier conditions
are D1–D4 (no authoritative reference; artifact survival not required), D5
(exact bytes if publication success returned), D6 (exact bytes plus
`STAGED_DURABLE`, no reference), D7 (wholly uncommitted or wholly committed),
and D8 (exact bytes, `COMMITTED` record, reference and owning row together).
This is the normative minimum; individual Android database columns beyond the
state and identity needed for that oracle remain producer-detail until mapped
to a frozen claim.

| Claim / emitted data | Current producer and host consumer | Existing executed evidence | P / F / C / E; finite remainder |
|---|---|---|---|
| Published bytes and hash: published_file_exists, published_file_length, published_file_sha256; fixture-bound per-run expected_payload_bytes/expected_sha256 | S1Diagnostic.capture hashes bytes from the filesystem and now opens the database strictly read-only. validate_dir requires exact equality between each external and embedded pre/post dump, validates hook-conditional publication and hash/length, and hashes the actual artifacts/S1/<operation_id>.bin for D5, D6, D8 and committed D7. D1–D4 and wholly uncommitted D7 do not require artifact survival. | Attempt 25 passes the complete affected S1/run-schema host group (251 pytest, 106 unittest). Attempts 24 and 26 pass isolated API 36.1 instrumentation for no-create/no-upgrade and DB query-error-vs-empty cases. | P: producer emits fixture-bound values. F/C: host validates snapshot shape, hook conditions, exact external↔embedded equality, expected vs observed fields and independent disk bytes. E: hash/open/query errors propagate; tests reach these branches. Remaining: final-candidate S1 DEV evidence and broader I10 aggregate/path mutations; no wider physical/QUAL claim. |
| Temporary state: temp_file_exists, temp_file_length | Producer reads the run temp file. The host validates Boolean/nonnegative integer shape, requires length zero when exists=false, and includes both values in exact external↔embedded dump equality. Frozen Protocol v1.1 §3.5 defines no temp-file lifecycle per barrier, and the values do not determine the S1 outcome. | Attempt 25 covers the current S1 host validator; no per-barrier temp lifecycle matrix was added because no frozen rule defines one. | P/F/C/E: present, typed and bound across evidence copies; lifecycle is intentionally not asserted beyond frozen protocol. Do not add a temp-survival rule. |
| artifact_record and its emitted fields (artifact_id, state, size_bytes, checksum, plus other database columns) | S1Diagnostic.capture opens only an existing database through SQLiteDatabase.OPEN_READONLY; record-query/serialization errors throw instead of becoming null. A successful no-row query yields null. S1OracleEvaluator and host/runner.py bind the fixture artifact_id and required D6/D7/D8 state. Record size_bytes/checksum are not made acceptance inputs absent a frozen claim. | Attempt 25: S1/run-schema host tests pass 251/106. Attempt 24 verifies missing DB is not created and v1 is not upgraded; attempt 26 verifies missing-table queries throw and provisioned empty tables return null/empty. | P/F/C/E: record/null distinction, fixture ID/state and query failure are enforced; actual published-file hash is independently checked. The remaining 100-repeat-per-barrier product DEV evidence is not provided by synthetic fixtures. |
| artifact_references[] and authoritative_pages[] with emitted artifact_id, ref_owner_id, ref_kind, protection_class, page_id, content_revision, created_at_ms | Queries use the read-only connection and propagate failures. D1–D6 reject premature authoritative rows; D7 accepts either no rows plus the fixture-bound staged record or exactly one complete committed set; D8 requires exact artifact ID, one reference and one owning row whose owner/page IDs agree. Other Android columns remain producer-detail unless frozen. | Host S1 matrix executes positive D1–D8 controls and isolated foreign/partial/duplicate row mutations in attempt 25. Attempts 24 and 26 separately validate read-only capture and query failure vs empty rows on the API 36.1 AVD. | P/F/C/E: successful empty queries are distinct from read errors; row count, artifact identity, owner linkage and atomicity are checked. Remaining: final-candidate 100-repeat-per-barrier device evidence; no physical/QUAL inference. |

Across the extracted standalone dumps/S1/<run_id>-pre/post.json and embedded
per-run dumps, host validation requires a typed snapshot, exact run/mode/type and
tuple identity, and exact JSON-object equality between external and embedded
pre/post copies. Thus published bytes/hash, record, references, owning rows and
temporary fields cannot diverge between the copies; hook-specific state and
actual file bytes are then evaluated separately by the S1 oracle. Attempt 25
covers the host checks. No physical/QUAL result is inferred from synthetic DEV
fixtures.

#### 11.5.3 Diagnostic database open must not create or migrate — revision 5.395

Frozen Protocol v1.1 §3.5 calls for exporting the read-only diagnostic state
after process death and before recovery mutation. Source inspection found that
`S1Diagnostic.capture` uses `new S1Database(context).getReadableDatabase()`.
Although `S1Database.onOpen` is neutral, `SQLiteOpenHelper` owns create/upgrade
callbacks; Android's API documents `getReadableDatabase()` as “Create and/or
open a database.” An absent DB may therefore be changed into an empty database
and then look like an empty observation. This is an in-scope implementation
omission in the frozen read-only requirement, not a new acceptance criterion.

Before code changes, the required fix and test are preregistered in plan
revision 5.395: open only the already-existing DB with
`SQLiteDatabase.OPEN_READONLY`; propagate absent, inaccessible, corrupt, and
query failures; prove an absent file remains absent; and prove a readable v1
schema is not upgraded by capture. Existing fully provisioned DB tables with
no matching rows remain a valid empty-state observation. The isolated
instrumentation result will be attached to `S1-STATE-ORACLE-01`; it cannot close
physical qualification or imply a process-death device result.


Execution result (plan revision 5.396): S1Diagnostic.capture now uses the
explicit read-only open and propagates failures. Attempt 24 passed
s1DiagnosticCaptureDoesNotCreateOrUpgradeDatabase (1/1) on
DocScanMvpTest_API_36.1; it observed that an absent file remained absent, a
provisioned empty database remained empty, and user_version=1 was unchanged.
Attempt 26 passed s1DiagnosticDatabaseReadFailureDoesNotBecomeEmptyState
(1/1) on the same AVD; missing-table queries raised and provisioned empty-table
queries returned null/empty. Attempt 25 passed 251 S1/run-schema/S6/audit
pytest tests and 106 validator/orchestrator/S5 unittest tests; static checks
passed. Raw commands and outputs are under attempts 23–26 in
execution/device_tests/ENG-B1B2-INVENTORY-01/.

This closes the read-only-open correction at the focused host/harness boundary.
It does not provide the final candidate's full S1 D1–D8 DEV run (100 repeats
per barrier), physical qualification, independent review or owner acceptance.

#### I23 source/test reconciliation — revision 5.370

The exact-source v1.7 host log shows the existing resume controls executing at
`execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/check-all-attempt-1/host-tests.log`:
`test_resume_attempt_history_read_error_preserves_file_before_device_io`,
`test_resume_end_to_end_flow`, missing cycle serial, corrupted evidence
preservation, invalid history before device I/O, S2 rejection and APK mismatch
(lines 19–56); the journal schema tests for invalid outcome/missing keys/root
type/emulator serial/valid DEV history run at lines 233–245. The retained suite
is 107/107 (line 301). This is execution evidence for those named test bodies,
not coverage of every journal/identity property.

Static gaps that remain within the same I23 class:

- `_validate_resume_attempts_data` requires `attempt_timestamp`, `spike`,
  `mode`, `serial`, and `outcome`, but validates only mode, outcome enum, and
  emulator/canonical-serial conditions. It does not parse timestamp, close the
  spike enum against the resumed spike, require a nonblank typed serial, or
  validate `executed_runs`/run IDs or relation to earlier attempts. QUAL
  history is loaded without a canonical serial argument (`runner.py:839–850`,
  `2659–2662`), so its serial comparison does not bind each prior attempt to
  the cycle serial at that point.
- `cmd_resume` accumulates fingerprint/hash/serial fields across JSON files
  until the first truthy tuple is complete. This can combine identity from
  different files. After that tuple becomes complete, later files are still
  parsed for corruption but their identity fields are not compared. `dut_id`
  is captured in `cycle_dut_id` but is not consumed. `cycle_mode` is checked
  only when present/truthy (`runner.py:2663–2702`, `2754–2767`). The effective
  mismatch test covers cycle serial versus device serial; no valid executed
  cycle APK-hash mismatch control was found, and no all-file identity
  cross-binding/order permutations are tested.
- Device fingerprint and `sha256sum` commands require rc 0 and nonempty output;
  `pm path` accepts any output containing a `package:` line without checking
  return code (`runner.py:2734–2752`). The parsed APK digest token is compared
  with the cycle value but is not independently required to be 64 lowercase
  hex (`2758–2764`). Existing tests cover missing fingerprint/hash-query and
  serial mismatch, not a valid cycle APK-hash mismatch, plausible package
  output with nonzero rc, or malformed matching digest tokens.
- The journal mutation controls cover root type, required-key omission,
  outcome enum, emulator serial, and unreadable-file preservation. They do not
  cover timestamp/spike/serial/`executed_runs` field types, blank/null values,
  duplicate run IDs across attempts, preservation of earlier records during a
  successful append/update, or new-attempt ID/reference linkage required by
  `docs/08_MVP_GATES_AND_ACCEPTANCE.md:51`.

**Consolidated regression contract candidate `I23-RESUME-IDENTITY-01`:** keep
the existing positive end-to-end and non-destructive history cases; add a
multi-file identity fixture with matching tuples in every JSON plus all order
permutations of split, later-conflicting, and missing identity fields; mutate
each journal field's type/value; seed repeated/mislinked `executed_runs`; and
make each ADB preflight mock return nonzero rc alongside plausible stdout.
Acceptance requires failure before any S1 execution on every mismatch, source
evidence preservation, and a truthful append/update that preserves prior
attempt records. Check that attempted writes do not drop or rewrite older
entries; this does not add a tamper-evident history requirement. Retain S2
rejection, ordinary missing-command failures, and exact identity comparison
as controls. No new retry or qualification scope is added.

No tests or device commands were run during this inventory refinement. The
listed gaps remain source candidates; existing passed test execution is reused
only for named paths above. Include this slice in the single I01–I25 batch.

### 11.8 Gate/task input and verdict links — I25

There are two separate ledgers by design. `execution/tasks.json` is the harness
subcommand input read by `host/runner.py::cmd_verify_gates`; it currently contains
12 gates and 11 harness tasks. `execution/product_tasks.json` is the product
MVP ledger and is read by its own validator through `tools/check_all.sh`, not by
`cmd_verify_gates`. `execution/traceability.csv` is the harness traceability
input; product `execution/traceability_product.csv` is separately validated.
This separation is not itself a conflict or permission to merge/status-edit the
ledgers.

| Exact path / input | Condition and source behavior | Existing regression/evidence | P / F / C / E |
|---|---|---|---|

| `$.tasks` and `$.tasks[]` | Missing defaults to `[]`; empty list is accepted. Entry object, required ID, known task set and unique ID are not validated as a schema; malformed entries may raise incidental exceptions. | Existing gate test deliberately uses `tasks: []` while asserting incomplete gates; no empty-all-ledger negative control. | TODO / TODO / TODO / TODO |

Traceability to source/test/run is not checked here; another validator/checklist owns that contract. | Status test covers gate states only; no missing/duplicate ID, malformed deps, false evidence text or dependency-cycle tests mapped for this command. | TODO / PASS only basic DONE predicate / TODO evidence-path/source/test/run binding / TODO |

The current test proves unknown/incomplete gate statuses do not become DONE and

#### 11.8.1 I25 source/test reconciliation — revision 5.372

**Baseline:** runner v1.7, `host/runner.py` SHA-256
`95b75edd16df18fc7ef939a79dc8202c319ae70d84d7fbd44ccc5162f97dcbab`, source
commit `fa4d7ad2`; product `.216` / versionCode 335. Source reading is against
the active isolated checkout. No test, build, package, ADB or device command
was run for this reconciliation.

| Boundary | Source result | Existing executed evidence | Remaining property/regression mapping |
|---|---|---|---|
| `execution/tasks.json` parse/root/containers | `cmd_verify_gates` parses JSON, then assumes an object. Missing `gates` and `tasks` default to `{}` and `[]`; both empty can reach the success message. Wrong root/container/entry types may raise incidental exceptions. The required B0–B9/Q/A gate ID set, uniqueness, task-to-gate links and complete task inventory are not validated. `docs/08` names this file as the harness task/gate registry; no separate authoritative harness task-ID catalogue was found. | `tests/test_gate_status_report.py::test_only_done_is_complete_and_strict_mode_fails_closed` supplies six gates and `tasks: []`; it verifies selected statuses, not empty-all or malformed schema. Exact-source `host-tests.log` records the test and the host suite at 107/107. | `I25-GATE-LEDGER-01`: full-ledger positive control; missing/empty/wrong-type root, `gates`, `tasks`, gate/task entries; missing/duplicate/unknown required gate IDs; task IDs, gate links, duplicate IDs, missing tasks and unknown dependency IDs. For each rejection require a diagnostic/nonzero acceptance result and forbid “all complete”. Use `execution/tasks.json` as the harness registry and `execution/traceability.csv` as its reciprocal reference check; don't infer the harness task set from product tasks. Simultaneous deletion from both files cannot be detected without another authoritative catalogue, and no such harness catalogue is identified in current sources. |
| Gate/task states and dependencies | Any gate state except exact `DONE` is listed incomplete; unknown states therefore fail closed as states. A DONE task needs nonempty `commit` and `evidence` strings, and each listed dependency must map to status DONE. It does not resolve commit/build identity, check source ID/code symbol/check command/exit/timestamp, resolve task evidence inside a package, or validate dependency graph/cycles. `tasks[].gate` is unused. | Existing status control verifies IN_PROGRESS, PARTIAL, NOT RUN, unknown, missing status; it exercises one DONE gate with a mocked evidence directory. It does not mutate task evidence, IDs, deps, gate links or graph structure. | Same batch: empty/fake evidence, malformed/unknown/cyclic dependencies, missing/duplicate task identity, unresolved gate/task association, and DONE evidence with missing frozen fields or broken references. Positive control must preserve a genuine complete graph and all existing non-DONE statuses. |
| Gate evidence path/mode | Evidence path must be nonempty and exist; `WORKSPACE / ev_pkg` is not resolved/contained before use. Its declared mode is validated internally, but is not bound to gate identity; therefore a DEV package can satisfy the current Gate Q evidence branch if the gate is marked DONE. | Existing status test mocks `_evidence_mode_from_package` and `validate_dir`; the retained `check_all` report validates the actual B0 DEV package, not a Gate Q package. `validate_dir` separately rejects emulator identity in QUALIFICATION, but does not bind a gate ID to its required mode here. | Same batch: traversal/absolute/symlink escape, absent/broken package, manifest mismatch, Q backed by DEV and Q backed by valid QUALIFICATION; preserve the original evidence tree on rejection. Required mode is QUALIFICATION for Gate Q; other gate-mode conditions must come from their existing contracts. |
| Harness traceability | `cmd_verify_gates` checks only that `execution/traceability.csv` exists. The actual `tools/check_all.sh` call to `execution/tools/validate_traceability.py` validates the separate product file `execution/traceability_product.csv` (CAP/BR/AC inventory), not the harness columns in `traceability.csv`. This leaves the `docs/08` registry/traceability claim without an executed harness-schema check. Static comparison finds 11 distinct harness task IDs versus 12 traceability task IDs: `TASK-Q-01` and `TASK-A-01` are referenced only by traceability, while `TASK-P1-01` exists only in the harness registry. This is a recorded source-data inconsistency, not proof of any gate passing. | Existing exact-source log reports product traceability PASS and contains a synthetic `traceability.csv` with only `id\n` in the status test. No direct missing/header-only/broken-reference harness CSV mutation was executed. The ID comparison above is a read-only inspection of both checked-in files, not a test. | Same batch: missing file, header-only, malformed columns/rows, duplicate source/task links, bidirectional task-ID resolution (including the three named mismatches), invalid task/evidence/test references and a complete positive harness mapping. Retain the product validator as its separate domain and do not merge the two CSV contracts. |
| Frozen hash registry | `verify_frozen_hashes` checks each configured path's existence and digest, but `{}` vacuously passes; exact required path inventory, object/digest schema, 64-hex format and workspace containment are not enforced. Malformed input can escape as an incidental exception. The current registry lists the two frozen documents. | Exact-source `check_all` records current frozen-hash verification. No executed empty/wrong-type/omitted-path/bad-digest/escaping-path mutation matrix was found in the mapped tests. | Same batch: complete positive registry; missing/empty/wrong-type registry; missing required frozen entry; invalid digest/path; changed/missing frozen file; read failure. Require specific diagnostics and nonzero acceptance result. |
| Strict vs diagnostic invocation | `verify-gates` currently defaults to diagnostic behavior; `--strict` returns 1 for incomplete gates/tasks. `tools/check_all.sh` intentionally calls it non-strict by default and prints that exit 0 means report/evidence validation only. This is useful for local checks, but the CLI default alone does not match `docs/08` §3's normative nonzero-on-incomplete rule. | `execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/check-all-attempt-1/gate-report-nonstrict.log` correctly prints 11 incomplete gates and 8 incomplete tasks while `check_all` succeeds. This is not an acceptance PASS. | Decision `ENG-VERIFY-GATES-STRICT-01`: make acceptance strict by default; retain an explicit report-only mode for the local `check_all` route. Test default nonzero on incomplete; report-only returns zero while printing every incomplete item and never claiming all complete; a fully valid positive ledger succeeds in both modes. |

**Evidence boundary:** the 107/107 run at runner v1.7 is reusable only for the
named existing tests. The source-derived conditions above were not reproduced
by a new test and are not independent review. The current non-strict report is
honest about its 11 gate/8 task remainder, but does not resolve empty-registry,

**Batch condition:** include I25 in the single consolidated runner regression
batch with aggregate schema/I13 repeat-matrix/common per-run coverage, I23
resume, I24 package transaction/path safety, all command-result boundaries and
the mapped frozen-file/manifest cases. Before code changes, finish every

this tooling slice requires addressable host regressions on one final source,
preservation checks for rejected evidence paths, a complete check-all run with
explicit report-only semantics, and the agreed DEV package validation. This
does not accept B1/B2, qualify Gate Q, replace independent review, or promote

The planned reconciliation produces 13 task records and 13 distinct reciprocal
traceability IDs: the current eleven tasks plus Q/A, and the current twelve CSV
rows plus P1. This count is an expected schema-control value, not evidence of
completion; all existing gate/task statuses remain untouched. The current
registry cannot detect a task deleted from both registry and CSV; the existing
plan's B0–B9/Q/A scope is the independent completeness reference for review.

**Regression ID `I25-TASK-RECIPROCITY-01`:** positive fixture has all 13
registered IDs, one row per task, correct gate/dependency links and Q/A
BLOCKED conditions; it preserves the P1 status while validating its full
source/evidence references. Negative controls remove each of the three links
in turn, reference an unknown task/gate, break Q/A dependencies, duplicate a
task/row, and mutate a DONE reference to absent or outside-package evidence.
Every negative must fail closed without printing an all-complete result. No
ledger or traceability data was changed and no tests ran during this source
reconciliation.

#### 11.9.3 I14–I20 state-dump schema and run binding — revision 5.381

Frozen Protocol §9 requires `pre_state_dump` and `post_state_dump` in every
S1–S6 per-run record and represents each as `null` in the schema template.
S1 also has standalone dumps and the stronger D1–D8 state oracle in §§3–3.5;
S5/S6 have their explicit nested state roots in §11.9.1. S2–S4 have no frozen
nested child schema beyond §9, so their existing `{}` producer values are
valid empty diagnostics and must not be expanded into new state requirements.

For S1, standalone dumps independently exist as files but still originate from
the app snapshot. The current reader cross-check is limited to five embedded
values and does not bind external SHA-256 or state entities; see §11.5.2. For
S2, `pages[]` is producer extension and its missing top-level state keys
already pass an executed INCOMPLETE fixture. S3/S4 empty objects occur in
executed positive fixtures. This source/log audit has no physical device or
QUALIFICATION dump proof.

**Batch condition `RUN-STATE-SCHEMA-01`:** one positive per spike with both
schema keys represented as null or valid object according to the frozen
template/producer; one-at-a-time missing and wrong-type mutations for the
top-level keys; preserve S1 D1–D8 state semantics, S5/S6 conditional state
validators, and S2's current checks. If `pages[]` remains emitted, mutations
must not be accepted as false committed-page evidence; do not mandate extra
member fields without a frozen requirement. Retain standalone dump path,
timestamp, run/type and artifact-byte controls. Require no new S3/S4 nested
data or measurement threshold.

### 11.11 Frozen aggregate consumer scan — candidate incomplete-result acceptance

A source walk of `host/runner.py::validate_dir` confirms that common aggregate
validation requires only `spike_id`, `qa`, `dut_id`, `device`, `app_build_hash`,
`raw_evidence_location`, `outcome`, and `execution_mode`, followed by selected
spike-specific counters and run matrices. Frozen result-template fields outside
those selected checks need explicit path validation or a documented conditional
exemption. Source locations: aggregate loop at `runner.py:1278+`; S2 at 1396+;
S3 at 1506+; S4 at 1566+; S5 at 1649+; S6 at 1763+.

| Frozen paths / condition | Source finding and existing positive/mutation evidence | Current classification and missing controls |
|---|---|---|
| S1 optional extension/report fields `$.hard_power_extension_executed`, `$.hard_power_method`, `$.hard_power_runs_completed`, `$.sudden_power_loss_qualified`; `$.publish_latency_ms_p50/p95/p99`; `$.first_reference_tx_ms_p50/p95/p99` | `build_s1_aggregate_from_disk` emits these paths (hard power as false/null/0, percentiles from measured lists); `validate_dir` validates S1 run count and failure counters but not these values/types or derivation. Existing S1 checks focus on kill counters and run completeness. | **Source gap candidate.** Preserve the optional extension condition: its execution is not required, but scope/status fields must exist truthfully. Add missing/null/wrong-type/invalid-percentile and per-run recomputation controls; do not impose an unstated latency threshold. |
| Frozen common aggregate paths `$.outcome`, `$.qa`, `$.qa[]` | `req_agg` requires the keys, but enum validation skips `outcome=null`; `qa` membership checks do not require the frozen array type. Protocol §2.5 defines the result vocabulary; the templates show QA as an array. No exact-target null-outcome or non-list QA mutation is mapped. | **Source-level schema gap.** Add valid outcome/QA controls and null/unknown outcome, string/object QA, missing, and malformed-array cases. Preserve the mode/gate distinction for `INCOMPLETE`; do not equate it with `null`. |
| S1 runner-added oracle extension `$.failed_runs_count`, `$.oracle_violations_count`, `$.oracle_violations` | These are **not fields in the frozen aggregate templates**; `S1Harness` emits them at lines 327–329 and the runner consumes them for an extra PASS-vs-oracle check. For PASS, missing values default to zero/empty in that extra check. They are not emitted by S3; therefore the S3 fixture's omission at host log line 277 is N/A for this S1-only extension, not a missing frozen field or S3 defect. `test_reject_aggregate_outcome_pass_with_oracle_failures` sets `failed_runs_count=1` and oracle evidence, but executed line 144 exits on failed runs before the oracle branch. | **Existing S1 extension test-target gap; scope remains implementation-only.** Preserve the S1 positive/negative behavior already asserted by the test comment, but set `failed_runs_count=0` in dedicated counter/list mutations so each oracle branch executes. Cross-check their producer relation only if required by the current S1 extension contract; do not add these fields to S2–S6 templates or qualification criteria. |
| S2 `$.workloads.C-W{0,1,2}.request_to_tx_commit_ms_{p50,p95,p99}` and `.request_to_page_visible_ms_{p50,p95,p99}` (18 workload/metric paths) | Validator checks ten named counters, their integer type and reconciliation to per-run counts; it does not read these 18 percentile paths or derive them from `$.measured_metrics`. `test_s2_valid_dev_evidence` is intentionally `INCOMPLETE` with null W1/W2, so it is not a positive complete-package control. | **Source gap candidate.** Add complete positive all-workload control; missing/null/non-finite/wrong-type values and independently recomputed source-set changes must fail closed. No absolute performance threshold is frozen. |
| S3 `$.ocr_adapter_version`, `$.max_input_width_px`, `$.max_input_height_px`, `$.accepted_formats`, `$.O-F1_latency_ms_p50/p95/p99`, `$.O-F2_latency_ms_p50/p95/p99`, `$.O-F3_latency_ms_p50/p95/p99`, `$.process_pss_kib_peak`, `$.process_rss_kib_peak` | S3 branch checks QA, `input_envelope_defined`, selected limits/counters, fixture counts and system-health flag, but no direct read of these listed frozen paths. `test_s3_aggregate_fail_closed_checks` creates a PASS aggregate without these fields and its completed fixture is accepted in the v1.7 host run (`execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/check-all-attempt-1/host-tests.log:277`, suite 107/107). | **Concrete validator false-acceptance candidate by executed positive fixture plus source.** Add one-field omission/type/unit tests, fixture-to-percentile/PSS/RSS derivation controls, and missing envelope field → INCOMPLETE/FAIL per frozen rule. No OCR accuracy or latency threshold is added. |
| S4 `$.pdf_composer_version`, `$.pdf_validator_version`, `$.host_validator_name_version`, `$.max_page_width_px`, `$.max_page_height_px`, `$.page_format`, `$.per_page_count_metrics` | S4 branch enforces `P_MAX`, selected fail counters, point counts, and PDF validation using per-run SaveManifest, but does not directly require/read these frozen aggregate fields. `test_s4_aggregate_and_host_pdf_validation` accepts a PASS aggregate missing them and per-run fixtures omitting SaveManifest `manifest_id` and `manifest_payload_hash`; it supplies page count, IDs and hashes only. Executed in exact v1.7 host run (`execution/device_tests/ENG-RUNNER-INSTRUMENTATION-EXIT-01/check-all-attempt-1/host-tests.log:283`, suite 107/107). | **Concrete validator false-acceptance candidate by executed positive fixture plus source.** Require recorded host/app validator identity and bounded raster/format precondition; map each point's composition metrics. Expand SaveManifest missing/type/order/hash mutations and check PDF ↔ manifest ↔ source run binding. No new P_MAX/raster threshold beyond frozen/product profile. |
| S5 `$.qualification_tuple_inventory_complete`, `$.tuples[]` and all 29 tuple member paths in §11.10 | `validate_dir` checks `provider_tuples_inventory_defined`; when false it requires `qualification_outcome == INCOMPLETE`. **Consolidated regression contract candidate `I13-QUAL-MATRIX-01`:**

- For every spike, build a complete qualification fixture at precisely the
  frozen matrix size and require it to validate; then remove one required cell,
  misassign one run to another workload/fixture/point/scenario/tuple/hook, and
  add one out-of-matrix run. Each invalid fixture must fail qualification.
- S2: preserve workload batch identity and prove 20 × 100 commands per
  workload, with each accepted `operation_id` tied through C1–C7 and explicit
  typed failure accounting for any non-accepted command.
- S3: bind 20 distinct 50-run batches per fixture, restart the app between
  batches, and prove all 1,000 runs; separately prove 500 contention sets and
  their admission observations.
- S4: require exactly 50 runs at each point and exactly 200 distinct P_MAX
  contention runs, with each output still subject to PDF/SaveManifest checks.
- S5: bind all four scenario counts to every frozen qualification tuple; require
  the declared tuple inventory to match the observed set and reject missing,
  duplicate, or unregistered tuple cells. Preserve the real-provider evidence
  limitation for known failure.
- S6: require exactly 100 unique runs for each R01–R41, while preserving all
  existing hook oracle, dump, uniqueness, and idempotency checks. Keep S1's
  existing exact-count implementation as a regression control.
- Preserve a separately identified DEV fixture/profile and ensure it cannot
  validate as QUALIFICATION. Do not infer a new DEV repetition threshold or
  alter any physical qualification count.

references above. Existing executed evidence remains limited to the v1.7
host-suite run recorded in §11.11; the executed S3/S4 fixtures are DEV and do
not prove QUAL count behavior. S2's current positive fixture is INCOMPLETE;
S5's positive fixture is DEV with tuple inventory undefined; S6's positive
pytest-style fixture is not collected by `tools/check_all.sh`. No tests, build,
ADB, package command, or device execution was run for this repeat-matrix pass.
Do not edit runner/tests until the complete I01–I25 inventory and consolidated
batch contract are finished; the proposed mutations belong to the single
aggregate/schema/matrix regression batch, not five separate micro-packages.
